Method: FLARE++复用FLARE自身的编码器来构建路由查询：利用学习到的潜种子触发一次额外的编码操作，将N个输入token聚合成M个输入条件化的查询；这些查询再决定如何压缩和重新分配同样的token。整个过程仅使用标准缩放点积注意力（SDPA）调用，保持显式低秩分解和O(NM)线性复杂度。同时提供了多GPU上下文并行实现，将输入token分片到各设备而不在单设备上汇总完整序列。

Result: 在标准PDE代理基准上，FLARE++相比固定查询的FLARE平均提升24%；在Long Range Arena上平均提升2.3个准确率点，整体性能具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 动态token路由是提升低秩注意力模型能力的有效途径，FLARE++证明了在不牺牲线性复杂度的前提下，通过输入条件化的查询生成可以显著改进固定查询方案的性能。

Abstract: Full self-attention is a strong token mixer for PDE surrogates on irregular domains, but its quadratic cost limits its use on high-resolution problems. Efficient latent-attention models such as the Fast Low-rank Attention Routing Engine (FLARE) avoid that cost by routing all N tokens through M << N learned latent queries, but those queries are parameters: once trained, the same learned query templates serve every input. We remove this restriction with FLARE++, a low-rank attention architecture with dynamic token routing. FLARE++ reuses FLARE's own encoder to build its routing queries: learned latent seeds drive one extra encode call that gathers the N input tokens into M input-conditioned queries, and those queries then determine how the same tokens are compressed and redistributed. This preserves FLARE's explicit low-rank factorization and linear O(NM) complexity, and expresses the complete routing operation with standard scaled dot-product attention (SDPA) calls alone. We also provide a multi-GPU context-parallel implementation that shards input tokens across devices without ever gathering the full token sequence on one of them. FLARE++ is competitive across a set of standard PDE surrogate benchmarks, improving on fixed-query FLARE by 24% on average, and it gains 2.3 points of average accuracy on Long Range Arena.

</details>


### [136] [Hierarchical Federated Transfer Learning in Digital Twin-Based Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11532)
*Qasim Zia,Saide Zhu,Haoxin Wang,Zafar Iqbal,Yingshu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向数字孪生车载自组网(DT-VANET)的分层联邦迁移学习(HFTL)框架，通过按车辆类型聚类并进行簇内联邦迁移学习，结合数据质量评分机制抵御恶意车辆，有效解决了联邦学习在数据异质性和数据稀疏性下的全局模型精度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在DT-VANET中虽能保护数据隐私，但面临车辆数据异质性和数据稀疏性的挑战，导致全局模型对不同车辆类型的预测精度不足。需要一种结合迁移学习的层次化方案来解决该问题。

Method: 1) 将联邦迁移学习(FTL)与车辆类型聚类相结合，提出分层联邦迁移学习(HFTL)框架；2) 构建DT-VANET框架，设计云服务器模型更新算法和簇内联邦迁移学习算法；3) 提出基于数据质量评分的机制，防止恶意车辆影响全局模型。

Result: 在真实数据集上进行了详细实验，从多个性能指标验证了所提算法的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，所提出的HFTL框架能够有效提升全局模型在不同车辆类型上的预测精度，同时通过数据质量评分机制增强了模型对恶意攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: In recent research on the Digital Twin-based Vehicular Ad hoc Network(DT-VANET), Federated Learning (FL) has shown its ability to provide data privacy. However, Federated learning struggles to adequately train a global model when confronted with data heterogeneity and data sparsity among vehicles, which ensure suboptimal accuracy in making precise predictions for different vehicle types. To address these challenges, this paper combines Federated Transfer Learning (FTL) to conduct vehicle clustering related to types of vehicles and proposes a novel Hierarchical Federated Transfer Learning (HFTL). We construct a framework for DT-VANET, along with two algorithms designed for cloud server model updates and intra-cluster federated transfer learning, to improve the accuracy of the global model. In addition, we developed a data quality score-based mechanism to prevent the global model from being affected by malicious vehicles. Lastly, detailed experiments on real-world datasets are conducted, considering different performance metrics that verify the effectiveness and efficiency of our algorithm.

</details>


### [137] [Robust Ambiguity Detection (RAD) From Model- and Feature-Space Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11541)
*Manya Singh,Mark T. Keane,Arjun Pakrashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了鲁棒歧义检测（RAD）框架，通过模型空间一致性和特征空间一致性两个互补指标来量化预测歧义，并用RAD图进行可视化，最后展示了RAD在拒绝预测（abstention）场景下的下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型应在允许的变化下保持预测一致性，但当模型被等价替换或输入受到轻微扰动时，预测可能发生显著变化，这种"预测歧义"在高风险决策场景中需要被识别和标记。然而，模型部署后歧义难以直接识别，因此需要有效的量化框架。

Method: RAD框架提出两个互补指标：模型空间一致性（Model-Space Consistency）和特征空间一致性（Feature-Space Consistency），构成RAD评分对（RAD Score-Pair），并通过RAD图可视化。在合成数据集（控制重叠程度）和真实数据集上评估，并展示基于RAD帕累托排序的样本放弃预测的端到端应用。

Result: 在合成数据集和多个真实数据集上评估了RAD框架；下游应用中，通过RAD帕累托排序选择最歧义的样本进行放弃预测，达到了与现有拒绝式方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: RAD为预测歧义提供了可解释的量化与可视化方案，能有效识别歧义来源并指导用户采取相应行动，且在放弃预测任务上表现与现有方法相当。

Abstract: Machine learning models should be robust, in the sense of remaining predictively consistent under permissible variations. A model's predictions should ideally remain unchanged when it is replaced by a functionally equivalent one, or when its inputs are subject to minor, admissible perturbations. If such changes alter a prediction significantly, then the prediction is "ambiguous" with respect to the model. Models should abstain from making such ambiguous predictions and/or should flag them for human inspection, especially in high-stakes decision-making scenarios. However, in practice, such ambiguity is not easy to identify once a model is deployed. Here, the Robust Ambiguity Detection (RAD) framework is advanced for quantifying predictive ambiguity using two complementary metrics: Model-Space Consistency and Feature-Space Consistency. These two scores, the RAD Score-Pair, visualised through the RAD Plot, provide an interpretable characterisation of the sources of ambiguity and the actions a user may consider in response. RAD is evaluated on synthetic datasets with systematically controlled overlap, as well as several real-world datasets where the level of ambiguity cannot be directly inspected. Finally, we demonstrate a downstream application of RAD where samples are ranked by their RAD Pareto-Rank and the most ambiguous are abstained from prediction, achieving performance comparable to existing rejection-based approaches.

</details>


### [138] [Certifying What Helps Customer-Return Timing: A Screen-and-Confirm Test for Conditioning Signals, and Why Decay Is Nearly Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11555)
*Sang Su Lee,Vineeth Loganathan,Shishir Dash,Vijay Raghavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"筛查-确认"协议（screen-and-confirm protocol）和一种无模型上限（model-free ceiling），用以严谨地评估额外协变量信号是否能改善时序点过程（TPP）中的事件时序预测。通过在三个公开基准（Amazon、Taobao、RetailRocket）和真实市场（Thumbtack）上进行验证，作者发现：连续时间的衰减模型（decay）几乎已经充分，而领域内不断增加的额外条件化特征要么是冗余的，要么甚至是有害的（在公开基准上统计上为无效，最多提升0.06 NLL）。核心贡献不在于"衰减有效"这一发现，而在于提供了一套可检验、可认证的工具，以及诚实评估中的读出/泄漏陷阱记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从业者不断向客户回访预测模型中加入越来越多的信号（生命周期价值、类别、最近频率、日历、地理等），TPP文献也随之加入协变量和外部协变量条件化的强度函数。但一个根本性问题悬而未决：这些额外的信号真的能改善时序预测吗？此前的无效假设缺乏可信度，因为模型本身可能根本没有能力发现信号——"特征X无帮助"只有在模型确实具备发现信号的能力时才是有意义的陈述。

Method: 作者做出两项贡献：（i）筛查-确认协议：通过正向控制（positive control）在已知强度下植入耦合，验证模型能否恢复该信号，从而使得真实数据上的零结果可以被解读为"没有信号"而非"方法太弱"；该控制对类别型和连续型编码均验证有效，并在真实时钟驱动数据集（NYC出租车时段）上测试。（ii）无模型上限：量化客户回访时序中可被点预测（point-predictable）的程度，结果表明任何协变量仅能解释个位数百分比的间隔方差——回访几乎是无记忆的（near-memoryless）。

Result: 在三个公开基准（Amazon、Taobao、RetailRocket）和一个真实市场（Thumbtack）上，作者认证了一个清晰的结果：连续时间衰减（即已知优于冻结强度模型的方案）几乎已经充分；在此之上叠加领域不断添加的条件化特征要么是冗余的（公开基准上统计零结果，至多0.06 NLL），要么是无效乃至轻微有害的（市场上）。

Conclusion: 作者明确声明并非声称发现了"衰减有效"这一事实（该事实早已为人所知），他们的贡献在于提供了将"条件化无帮助"转化为可检验、可认证命题的工具，以及诚实评估过程中遇到的读出/泄漏陷阱的记录。

Abstract: Practitioners enrich customer-return models with ever more signals (lifetime value, category, recency/frequency, calendar, geography), and the temporal-point-process (TPP) literature follows suit with covariate- and external-covariate-conditioned intensities. But does any of it improve the timing, and how would you know? A null ("feature X doesn't help") is only meaningful if the model could have found a signal. We make two contributions--a method and a measurement--to answer this credibly. (i) A screen-and-confirm protocol that certifies whether a candidate signal improves a TPP's event-timing likelihood: a positive control plants a coupling of known strength and confirms the model recovers it, so a real-data null can be read as "no signal" rather than "weak method." The control is validated for categorical and continuous encodings, and on a real clock-driven dataset (NYC taxi hour-of-day). (ii) A model-free ceiling quantifying how little of customer-return timing is point-predictable at all (a single-digit percentage of gap variance from any covariate; returns are near-memoryless). With these we certify a clean result on three public benchmarks (Amazon, Taobao, RetailRocket) and a real marketplace (Thumbtack): the inter-event clock--continuous-time decay, long known to beat frozen-intensity models--is nearly sufficient, and the conditioning the field keeps adding is redundant or harmful on top of it (statistically null on the public benchmarks, at most 0.06 NLL; null to mildly harmful on the marketplace). We do not claim to discover that decay helps; our contribution is the tools that turn "conditioning doesn't help" into a checkable, certified statement--plus an honest-evaluation account of the read-out/leakage pitfalls we hit and retracted.

</details>


### [139] [When Offline Evaluation Misleads: A Diagnostic Protocol for Reward and Policy Selection in Delayed-Feedback Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11560)
*Sang Su Lee,Vineeth Loganathan,Shishir Dash,Vijay Raghavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个有序的诊断协议，用于在延迟反馈条件下评估情境多臂老虎机（CMAB）营销个性化系统，该协议从"对齐性"和"可学习性"两个维度筛选奖励与策略候选，并揭示了两个反复出现的教训：单一离线指标可能错误排序奖励信号，以及个性化溢价可能被高估为鲁棒性而非真正的个性化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销消息个性化使用CMAB可以带来实际商业价值，但真正重要的下游转化目标（如转化率）通常数周后才能观察到，太晚而无法驱动在线学习。因此团队使用快速代理奖励训练bandit，但离线检查方法在延迟反馈下会系统性误导决策，需要更可靠的诊断方法。

Method: 作者设计了一个有序的诊断协议，从两个轴对奖励和策略候选进行筛选：(1) 对齐性（alignment）——优化该奖励是否真正推动北极星指标；(2) 可学习性（learnability）——bandit能否识别出奖励最优策略。该协议在离策略评估公开基准和可控制的合成生成器上验证，并应用于大规模市场推送系统。

Result: 研究发现两个反复出现的教训：(N1) 单一离线数值可能错误排序奖励——更密集的奖励信号给bandit更多学习空间，静态估计中看似持平的奖励在在线学习中会分化；(N2) 如果无法预先知道哪种单一消息最优，逐用户策略可能只是避免押注错误选择，这看起来像个性化实为鲁棒性，导致"个性化溢价"被高估。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是方法论层面而非算法层面：提出了有序的诊断协议，揭示了两个关键教训，并分享了在延迟反馈CMAB系统中端到端应用该协议的经验。在五臂单次分割的大规模部署系统中，证据是方向性的而非统计功效充分的。

Abstract: Personalizing marketing messages with contextual multi-armed bandits (CMABs) drives real business value, yet the objective that ultimately matters - a downstream conversion - is observed only weeks later, too late to drive online learning. Teams therefore train the bandit on a fast proxy reward, and separately must judge whether a contextual bandit is worth its complexity over sending one best message. Settling both decisions with the usual offline checks - a batch off-policy estimate, a marginal arm-discrimination test, a confidence interval - can mislead systematically under delayed feedback. We give an ordered diagnostic protocol that screens a reward-and-policy candidate on two axes, alignment (does optimizing the reward move the north-star?) and learnability (can the bandit identify the reward-optimal policy?), before trusting any reported lift. We validate it where the truth is known - a public off-policy-evaluation benchmark and a controllable synthetic generator - and illustrate it on a deployed large-marketplace push system (where, with five arms and one split, the evidence is directional rather than powered). Two lessons recur. (N1) A single offline number can mis-rank rewards: a denser reward signal gives the bandit more to learn from, so rewards that look tied in a static estimate pull apart once learning happens online. (N2) If you cannot tell in advance which single message is best, a per-user policy partly just avoids betting on the wrong one - that looks like personalization but is really robustness, so a "personalization premium" is easily overstated. Our contribution is methodological rather than algorithmic: the ordered protocol, the two lessons it surfaces, and the end-to-end experience of applying it to a delayed-feedback CMAB.

</details>


### [140] [Sparse and robust geometric twin support vector machine via asymmetric RoBoSS loss function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11567)
*Kai Qi,Xinji Huang,Hongchun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非对称鲁棒有界稀疏平滑(aR)损失函数的L1范数惩罚几何孪生支持向量机(aRSGTSVM)，用于同时处理分类和回归任务。该方法通过L1范数实现特征选择，通过aR损失函数缓解标签噪声和重采样噪声的影响，并利用影响函数进行了鲁棒性统计分析。由于优化问题是非凸非光滑的，文章开发了基于近端梯度下降的快速稳定求解算法。在合成数据集、UCI数据集以及中国股市指数跟踪任务上的实验表明，该方法优于相关的先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中的训练数据通常包含冗余特征、标签噪声和特征噪声，这对机器学习方法的效率提出了严峻挑战。标准SVM采用L2范数惩罚和铰链损失函数，缺乏特征选择能力且对噪声敏感。因此需要一种既能实现特征选择又能同时抵御标签噪声和特征噪声的鲁棒分类方法。

Method: (1) 提出一种非对称、鲁棒、有界、稀疏且平滑的aR损失函数；(2) 将该损失函数与L1范数惩罚结合到几何孪生SVM框架中，构建aRSGTSVM模型；(3) 利用影响函数对模型的鲁棒性进行统计分析；(4) 由于模型涉及非凸非光滑优化，开发了基于近端梯度下降的快速稳定求解算法。

Result: 在合成数据集和UCI数据集上与相关先进方法相比，aRSGTSVM表现出优越性。此外，在中国股市不同指数的跟踪任务中，该方法也取得了令人满意的表现。

Conclusion: 提出的aRSGTSVM通过创新的aR损失函数和L1范数惩罚，有效解决了标准SVM在特征选择和噪声鲁棒性方面的不足，在分类、回归和实际的指数跟踪应用中均展现了优异的性能。

Abstract: In real-world scenarios, the training data usually contains redundant features, label noise and feature noise, which provide severe challenges for the efficiency of machine learning methods. Since standard support vector machine (SVM) adopts $l_2$-norm penalty and hinge loss function, it lacks the ability of selecting significant features and is sensitive to noise. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel asymmetric, robust, bounded, sparse and smooth (aR) loss function for $l_1$-norm penalized geometric twin SVM (aRSGTSVM) to handle classification and regression tasks. The $l_1$-norm penalty can achieve the feature selection. The proposed aR loss function can not only effectively mitigate the impact of label noise, but also significantly enhance the stability to resampling noise, i.e., the zero-mean feature noise around the boundary hyperplanes. Furthermore, a statistical analysis of the robustness of aRSGTSVM was also conducted using the influence function. Since aRSGTSVM involves nonconvex and nonsmooth optimization, we develop a fast and stable proximal gradient descent based solving algorithm. Compared with related state-of-the-art methods, experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed aRSGTSVM on both synthetic and UCI datasets. Furthermore, we apply aRSGTSVM to index tracking tasks, where results for tracking the different indices in the China stock market show that it can achieve satisfactory performance.

</details>


### [141] [RECAST: A Machine-Learning Framework for Correction and Super-Resolution of Coarse-Grid PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11572)
*Maryam Reza,Farbod Faraji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAST是一个机器学习框架，通过在学习校正粗网格数值时间步进的同时重建细网格状态，在不增加计算成本的前提下恢复粗网格PDE模拟的精度，在六类一维PDE系统中将时间平均相对误差降低50-92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗网格数值求解器可以显著降低时变PDE模拟的计算成本，但分辨率不足会导致轨迹和空间保真度下降。现有方法难以在维持粗网格演化的同时恢复丢失的精度。

Method: RECAST结合两类学习模块：(1) 在数值时间步进循环内进行学习校正，修正粗网格演化中的误差；(2) 从校正后的粗历史中重建对应的细网格状态，实现空间超分辨。

Result: 在六个一维PDE系统（涵盖输运、扩散、色散、反应和波动动力学）上，使用8-16倍粗化网格和1000步闭环推演，RECAST将时间平均相对误差较未校正粗网格求解器降低约50-92%；对未见PDE参数具有泛化能力；在5000步推演中优于当代粗校正架构。

Conclusion: RECAST证明了学习校正和重建能力可以使PDE演化在显著更粗的网格上进行而不损失解保真度，为面向高维数值模拟的机器学习加速提供了概念验证路径。

Abstract: Coarse-grid numerical solvers can substantially reduce the computational cost of time-dependent PDE simulation, but under-resolution often degrades both the trajectory and the spatial fidelity of the solution. We introduce RECAST (Recurrent Error Correction And Super-resolution of coarse-grid Trajectories), a machine-learning framework designed to restore this lost accuracy while retaining coarse-grid evolution. RECAST combines learned correction within the numerical time-stepping loop with reconstruction of the corresponding fine-grid state from the corrected coarse history. We evaluate the framework on six one-dimensional PDE systems spanning transport, diffusion, dispersion, reaction, and wave dynamics, using spatial grids coarsened by factors of 8-16 and 1000-step closed-loop rollouts from unseen initial conditions. Across the test cases, RECAST remains closely aligned with the fine-grid reference solutions and reduces time-averaged relative error by approximately 50-92% compared with the corresponding uncorrected coarse-grid solvers. Additional tests show generalization to unseen PDE parameter values, while comparison with a contemporary coarse-correction architecture shows that RECAST achieves lower error and better long-horizon agreement with the fine-grid reference over 5000-step rollouts. These results demonstrate that the learned correction and reconstruction capabilities of RECAST can enable substantially coarser PDE evolution without the corresponding loss of solution fidelity, providing a proof-of-concept route toward machine-learning acceleration of higher-dimensional numerical simulations across science and engineering.

</details>


### [142] [Dion3: Full-Stack Orthogonal Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11612)
*Noah Amsel,Jack Zhang,Kwangjun Ahn,Ali Naeimi,Austin Feng,Berlin Chen,Tri Dao,John Langford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dion3是对Muon优化器的改进版本，通过Gram Newton-Schulz算法降低正交化FLOP成本、利用CuteDSL内核加速对称性计算、采用megabatching策略减少通信开销，并通过对动量矩阵行进行部分正交化进一步降低计算成本，在保持或提升训练效果的同时将优化器步进时间减少最多6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器因其三次时间复杂度的Newton-Schulz正交化步骤带来显著计算开销，且当权重分片（sharding）时通信开销叠加，进一步侵蚀了Muon在多种场景下的收益。作者旨在从系统栈的各个层面系统性降低Muon的优化器开销，使其在各设置下都能发挥优势。

Method: 1) 提出Gram Newton-Schulz算法降低正交化步骤的FLOP成本；2) 利用CuteDSL kernel通过利用对称性加速计算；3) 提出megabatching策略减少通信开销；4) 修改更新规则，每步仅选择动量矩阵的一部分行进行正交化（压缩策略），相较于之前的Dion（另一版压缩版Muon）在速度和性能上均有提升。

Result: Dion3在损失值上与Muon相当或更优，同时将优化器步进时间降低最多6倍，并已作为Muon的即插即用替代方案开源。

Conclusion: Dion3通过多层次的系统优化（算法、内核、通信、更新规则）有效解决了Muon优化器的开销瓶颈，在不牺牲训练效果的前提下显著加速优化器执行，为大规模模型训练提供了更高效的优化器方案。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer incurs a significant overhead cost due to its cubic-time Newton-Schulz orthogonalization step. When weights are sharded, communication overhead compounds this computational cost, eroding the benefits of Muon in many settings. We present Dion3, a revision of Muon that targets this overhead at every level of the stack. Our Gram Newton-Schulz algorithm reduces the FLOP cost of orthogonalization, our CuteDSL kernels accelerate it by exploiting symmetry, and our megabatching strategy reduces communication overhead. Moreover, we propose a simple change to the update rule that cuts costs even further: selecting only a fraction of the momentum matrix's rows to orthogonalize at each step. This update rule improves on Dion (another "compressed" version of Muon), in both speed and performance. Overall, Dion3 matches or improves on the loss achieved by Muon but reduces optimizer step time by up to 6x. Dion3 is available via the dion package (https://github.com/microsoft/dion) as a drop-in replacement for Muon.

</details>


### [143] [A Local Sinkhorn Framework for Conditional Distribution Reconstruction of Multidimensional Random Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11613)
*Mingtao Xia,Qijing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于去偏Sinkhorn散度的局部分布匹配框架，用于多维随机场的条件分布重构，通过训练随机神经网络实现可微分且计算高效的分布匹配，并给出了泛化误差理论界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维随机场的条件分布重构在不确度量化与概率科学机器学习中至关重要，但精确局部最优传输方法计算代价高昂，难以扩展到高维场景。需要一种兼顾几何保真度、统计效率与计算可扩展性的替代方案。

Method: 提出局部Sinkhorn散度框架，利用去偏Sinkhorn散度构造可微分的局部分布匹配目标，用于训练随机神经网络（SNNs）。同时建立理论泛化误差估计，刻画正则化参数对近似偏差与统计效率之间的权衡。

Result: 通过多个数值实验，将提出的局部Sinkhorn散度框架与其他损失函数训练的SNN及其他基于机器学习的UQ框架进行比较，表明该框架在重构精度与计算效率之间取得了有效平衡，并对多维随机系统保持良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架为精确局部最优传输提供了可扩展的替代方案，在几何保真度、统计效率和计算可扩展性之间实现了实际可行的折中，适用于不确定度量化与概率科学机器学习。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a local Sinkhorn divergence framework for conditional distribution reconstruction of multidimensional random fields. By utilizing the debiased Sinkhorn divergence, our proposed approach develops a differentiable and computationally efficient local distribution matching objective to train stochastic neural networks (SNNs). Furthermore, we establish theoretical generalization error estimates for our local Sinkhorn divergence framework, which explicitly characterizes the trade-off between approximation bias and statistical efficiency controlled by the regularization parameter and reveals how our proposed local Sinkhorn divergence loss function can be efficiently applied to learning multidimensional random field models. The proposed framework provides a scalable alternative to exact local optimal transport for conditional distribution reconstruction, offering a practical compromise between geometric fidelity, statistical efficiency, and computational scalability for uncertainty quantification and probabilistic scientific machine learning. Through various numerical examples, we compare our proposed local Sinkhorn divergence framework with other loss functions to train SNNs and with other machine-learning-based uncertainty quantification frameworks, demonstrating that the proposed local Sinkhorn divergence framework achieves an effective balance between reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency while maintaining good scalability for multidimensional stochastic systems.

</details>


### [144] [FM-LLM: A frequency-enhanced mixture-of-experts framework for adapting LLMs to time series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11623)
*Rentao Gu,Yihang Ding,Junjie Li,Yi Ding,Weijing Sang,Xiaoli Huo,Xin Qin,Yuefeng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FM-LLM，一种基于傅里叶分析与混合专家（MoE）的框架，无需文本提示即可将冻结的LLM适配到时间序列预测任务。通过在频谱域注入谐波表示并进行非对称MoE解码，FM-LLM在11个公开基准的78项指标中取得59项最优，相比最强自回归LLM基线平均提升MSE 5.3%、MAE 5.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态时间序列预测方法过度依赖文本提示进行模态对齐，不仅引入大量计算开销，还未能充分利用时间序列本身丰富的频谱动态信息。作者希望提出一种无需提示、频率感知的轻量级方法，将冻结LLM高效适配到时间序列预测任务。

Method: FM-LLM采用自回归框架，基于约束非对称耦合：1）傅里叶分析网络（FAN）作为谱标记对齐器，将结构化谐波表示直接注入冻结LLM，实现数值兼容；2）非对称MoE解码器进行角色分离——共享专家搭配轻量FAN层重建全局周期主干，路由专家（仅限标准FFN）专门建模非周期残差动态；3）时频混合损失函数联合优化时间精度与频谱一致性，缓解长时序自回归滚动过程中的误差累积。

Result: 在11个公开基准的78项评估指标中，FM-LLM取得59项最优。相比最强自回归LLM基线，平均提升MSE 5.3%、MAE 5.6%，最大提升MSE达8.0%、MAE达8.4%。在10%少样本和零样本预测场景中仍保持优越性能，展现出良好的可迁移性。

Conclusion: FM-LLM通过频率增强与MoE角色分离机制，成功实现了无需文本提示的LLM时间序列预测适配，同时兼顾了预测精度、计算效率与模型泛化能力，为冻结LLM在时序预测领域的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have spurred cross-modal solutions for time-series forecasting. However, existing methods rely heavily on textual prompts for modality alignment-introducing nontrivial computational overhead and failing to leverage the rich spectral dynamics inherent in time-series data. To enable prompt-free, frequency-aware adaptation of frozen LLMs, we propose FM-LLM (Frequency-Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts for adapting LLMs to Time Series Forecasting), an autoregressive framework grounded in constrained asymmetric coupling. A Fourier Analysis Network (FAN)-based spectral token aligner injects structured harmonic representations directly into the frozen LLM with numerical compatibility. An asymmetric Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoder enforces role separation: shared experts with lightweight FAN layers reconstruct the global periodic backbone, while routed experts-restricted to standard FFNs-specialize in modeling non-periodic residual dynamics. A time-frequency hybrid loss function jointly optimizes temporal accuracy and spectral consistency, mitigating error accumulation during long-horizon autoregressive rollouts. Evaluated across eleven public benchmarks, FM-LLM achieves state-of-the-art performance on 59 out of 78 evaluation metrics. Compared to the strongest autoregressive LLM-based baseline, it delivers average improvements of 5.3% in MSE and 5.6% in MAE, with maximum gains reaching 8.0% for MSE and 8.4% for MAE. FM-LLM also demonstrates robust transferability, maintaining superior performance in 10% few-shot and zero-shot forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [145] [Transferable Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) Estimation Model from Multi-Sensor Data with Sparse Field Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11638)
*Pann Thinzar Seint,Bryan Atwood,Subas Chhatkuli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个操作化的森林地上生物量（AGB）估算框架，核心是一个全局训练的CNN模型，结合Sentinel-1/2、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2和DEM数据，通过GEDI Level-4A生物量参考数据训练，再通过轻量的实地样地校准流程（尺度与偏差校正+随机森林微调）适应局地景观，将未校准全局模型的R²约0.78/RMSE约22 Mg/ha提升至校准后的R²约0.82/RMSE约15 Mg/ha，优于ESA CCI Biomass产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林AGB的空间连续量化对碳核算至关重要，但野外样地数据空间稀疏，GEDI LiDAR虽提供大范围生物量样本却缺乏空间连续性且系统性低估高生物量森林。需要建立一种既具有全球泛化能力又能通过少量局地样地高效校准的操作化框架。

Method: 单一全局CNN模型，融合Sentinel-2光学、Sentinel-1 C波段SAR、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2 L波段SAR和DEM地形数据。模型基于多区域、旱雨季的GEDI Level-4A数据一次性训练，学习持久木质结构特征。数据在10m网格上统一，提取植被指数和极化比值，使用对数域SmoothL1+RMSE混合损失函数应对偏态分布。局地校准采用少量样地拟合尺度-偏差校正，并结合随机森林10折交叉验证微调消除区域偏差。

Result: 全局GEDI模型在预留验证集上R²约0.78、RMSE约22 Mg/ha；经场外实地校准后R²提升至约0.82、RMSE降至约15 Mg/ha，在实地样地上优于未校准的全局模型和ESA CCI Biomass产品。

Conclusion: 该框架证明通过"全局预训练+局地轻量校准"的范式可以兼顾AGB估算的全球一致性和局地精度，且无需为每个景观重新训练模型，为可操作的碳核算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Spatially continuous quantification of forest above-ground biomass (AGB) is what makes carbon accounting credible and mitigation strategies actionable. While field inventories provide high localized accuracy, they are spatially sparse; conversely, spaceborne LiDAR from the Global Ecosystem Dynamics Investigation (GEDI) offers broad biomass samples but lacks spatial continuity and systematic underestimation of high-biomass forests. This paper presents an operational framework centered on a single globally trained convolutional neural network (CNN) that is seamlessly adapted to each new landscape through a lightweight empirical field-calibration workflow. The global model combines optical (Sentinel-2), C-band SAR (Sentinel-1), L-band SAR (ALOS-2 PALSAR-2), and terrain (DEM) data. It is trained once against GEDI Level-4A biomass reference data spanning multiple regions and both wet and dry seasons so that it learns the persistent woody-structure rather than a single-date appearance. To avoid retraining for every landscape, the framework applies a small number of local field plots to fit a scale-and-bias correction that aligns the global prediction with ground truth in each region. The pipeline harmonizes sensor data onto a shared 10 m grid, derives vegetation indices and polarimetric ratios, computes per-band normalization stats, and trains the CNN with a hybrid log-domain SmoothL1 with RMSE loss for skewed biomass distribution. On held-out validation the global GEDI-based model achieved R^2 approximately 0.78 and RMSE approximately 22 Mg/ha. A subsequent field calibration combining Random Forest fine-tuning under a 10-fold cross-validation eliminates localized regional biases. This improves local validation performance to R^2 approximately 0.82 and reduces RMSE to approximately 15 Mg/ha, outperforming both the uncalibrated global model and the ESA CCI Biomass product against field plots.

</details>


### [146] [Towards a Formal Definition of Agent Memory: Basis, Span, Optimality, and the Sequential Memory Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11654)
*Hongyao Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为大型模型智能体的记忆提供了统一的形式化框架，将记忆定义为基（basis）、知识定义为其张成空间（span）、可回答性定义为覆盖问题，并引入效用-容量前沿作为衡量记忆系统优劣的统一标尺，同时在Homer史诗《奥德赛》上进行了具体实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管记忆在大型模型智能体中被广泛部署，但缺乏对"什么是记忆"以及"何时最优"的统一形式化描述，导致不同记忆系统之间无法进行公平比较，也难以指导记忆的构建与学习。

Method: （1）将记忆定义为基，知识定义为其张成空间，可回答性定义为覆盖问题：智能体存储从材料中提取的事件，生成算子将事件集转化为其蕴含的知识，查询可回答当且仅当张成空间中某个单项覆盖它。（2）最优记忆定义为容量约束下期望覆盖的最大化器，其价值构成效用-容量前沿。（3）引入噪声分析，探讨覆盖与精度的权衡，类比生物记忆的连续形成过程。（4）在顺序MDP中形式化持续智能体记忆问题，覆盖多个层次，其中记忆为状态、写为动作、查询时的效用为驱动学习的延迟奖励。（5）在《奥德赛》上进行实例化，将前沿、压缩区和覆盖与精度的分歧转化为具体数字。（6）将现有系统置于框架中定位。

Result: 通过将记忆问题重构为数学上可操作的形式化框架，作者在《奥德赛》上生成了具体数值结果（前沿、压缩区、覆盖与精度分歧），并成功将现有记忆系统映射到该框架中，使"记忆有多好"变得可测量。

Conclusion: 该框架使得记忆的优劣可量化度量，并将构建和学习智能体记忆的开放问题重新表述为具体的研究问题，为进一步的理论研究和系统设计提供了统一的基础。

Abstract: Despite the wide deployment of memory in large-model agents, there is no unified formal account of what a memory is or when it is optimal. This paper takes a first step toward this account. The central idea is that memory is a basis, knowledge is its span, and answerability is a coverage problem: an agent stores events extracted from a material; a generation operator turns any event set into the knowledge it entails; and a query is answerable exactly when some single item in the span covers it. The optimal memory is then the capacity-constrained maximizer of expected coverage, and its value traces a utility--capacity frontier, the common yardstick on which memory systems can be compared. Next, we consider noise in the memory and discuss coverage versus precision under it: a memory may store false claims, so the write policy must infer the truth of what it stores. Drawing an analogy with biological memory, which is formed continuously through ongoing experience, we formalize the continual agent-memory problem in a sequential MDP that covers multiple levels, where memory is the state, writing is the action, and the utility settled at query time is the delayed reward that drives learning. To make the framework concrete, we instantiate it on Homer's \emph{Odyssey}, turning the frontier, the compression zone, and the divergence of coverage from precision into concrete numbers. Finally, we position existing systems within the framework, making ``how good is a memory'' measurable and recasting the open problems of constructing and learning agent memory as concrete research questions.

</details>


### [147] [Continuous-Latent Predictive Modeling with Semantic Alignment for EEG-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11656)
*Myeong-Ju Cho,Hye-Bin Shin,Seo-Hyun Lee,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出脑潜在预测模型（BLPM），一种EEG-语言基础模型，通过将异构EEG解码任务重构为连续语义嵌入预测问题，实现跨主体、环境和数据集的泛化性神经解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型的预训练范式存在关键挑战：掩码自编码倾向于优先重构低级信号而非任务相关语义；自回归建模在连续神经动态与离散词元空间之间产生不匹配。因此需要新策略来有效对齐连续EEG表征与自然语言语义并集成大语言模型。

Method: BLPM提出两个核心模块：(1) 连续EEG潜在预测（CELP）编码器，通过潜在目标预测学习可迁移表征；(2) 多查询语义分解（MQSD）模块，提取任务相关信息并将连续EEG表征与文本语义在共享潜在空间中按语义关系对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多种任务中表现出一致的泛化性能，验证了连续潜在语义预测作为EEG-语言基础模型的有效范式。

Conclusion: 连续潜在语义预测为EEG-语言基础模型提供了一种有效的新范式，解决了现有预训练范式在语义对齐和表征可迁移性方面的局限。

Abstract: Recent advances in EEG foundation models have demonstrated the potential of large-scale pretraining to enable generalizable neural decoding across subjects, recording environments, and datasets. However, dominant pretraining paradigms face key challenges: masked autoencoding tends to prioritize low-level signal reconstruction over task-relevant semantics, while autoregressive modeling creates a mismatch between continuous neural dynamics and discrete token spaces. To address these challenges, new strategies are needed to effectively align continuous EEG representations with natural-language semantics and enable their integration with large language models. Accordingly, we propose Brain Latent Predictive Model (BLPM), an EEG-language foundation model that reformulates heterogeneous EEG decoding tasks as a continuous semantic embedding prediction problem. BLPM introduces a Continuous EEG Latent Predictive (CELP) encoder that learns transferable representations through latent target prediction. Building on these representations, a Multi-Query Semantic Decomposition (MQSD) module extracts task-relevant information and aligns continuous EEG representations with textual semantics within a shared latent space according to their semantic relationships. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent generalization performance across diverse tasks, establishing continuous latent semantic prediction as an effective paradigm for EEG-language foundation models.

</details>


### [148] [Is Per-Agent Policy Composition Safe? Rethinking Successor-Feature Transfer in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11658)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体强化学习中的目标动态变化问题，证明了独立组合策略库（每个智能体独立重组自己的库）可能产生比库中所有策略都差的联合行为，因为重组队友改变了每个智能体面临的环境。论文提出MA-USFA分层方法（下层为通用后继特征近似器，上层为跨智能体合成器），实现安全性与灵活性的统一，训练一次即可在部署时无需逐任务适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多强化学习系统（如车队管理、交通信号控制）在部署后目标会动态变化，而针对每个新目标重新训练策略代价过高。单智能体场景下，后继特征与广义策略提升及通用扩展可以解决此问题，但多智能体迁移受到的关注很少。常见的做法是让每个智能体独立重组自己的策略库，这会继承方法却丢失性能保证，可能产生比库中任何策略都差的联合行为。

Method: 论文首先理论证明独立组合在多智能体场景下会失效，因为重组队友改变了每个智能体面对的环境，使其依赖的价值函数失效（这是单智能体场景没有的失败模式）。进而证明唯一无条件安全的固定规则是同步组合（整个团队切换到联合训练策略），但该方法无法服务于给不同智能体分配不同目标的目标函数。为此提出MA-USFA：一个两层级层次化方法，下层为通用后继特征近似器，在给定队友目标的条件下预测每个智能体的后继特征；上层合成器跨智能体选择每个智能体应遵循的库条目，并提供单智能体价值无法表达的跨智能体校正。该方法在目标分布上训练一次，部署时无需逐任务适配。

Result: 论文给出了多智能体策略库组合中独立组成会产生严格更差联合行为的理论证明，论证了同步组合是唯一无条件安全的固定规则但其灵活性受限，并提出了兼具安全性与灵活性的MA-USFA方法。

Conclusion: 在多智能体情况下，简单地在每个智能体上独立应用单智能体的后继特征组合技巧是不够的，需要显式地表征队友目标和跨智能体交互。MA-USFA通过分层架构（预测各智能体条件后继特征的下层+跨智能体选择与校正的上层）同时实现了安全性和灵活性，且训练一次即可部署，无需逐任务适应。

Abstract: Many reinforcement learning systems, from fleet management to traffic signal control, must serve an objective that changes dynamically after deployment, and retraining a policy for each new objective is prohibitively expensive. For a single agent, this problem is well understood: successor features with generalized policy improvement, together with their universal extension, recombine a library of learned policies into a policy for any new objective, with a guarantee that the result is never worse than any policy in the library. However, multi-agent transfer has received far less attention, and the common practice of letting each agent recombine its own library independently inherits the recipe but not the guarantee. We prove that this independent composition can produce joint behavior strictly worse than every policy in the library, because recombining teammates changes the environment each agent faces and invalidates the values it relies on, a failure with no single-agent counterpart. We further show that the only unconditionally safe fixed rule is synchronized composition, which moves the whole team to one jointly trained policy but cannot serve objectives that assign different goals to different agents. To attain safety and flexibility at once, we propose MA-USFA, a hierarchical method with two layers: a lower layer of universal successor feature approximators that predicts each agent's successor features while conditioned on its teammates' objectives, and an upper composer that selects, across agents, which library entry each agent should follow and supplies the cross-agent correction a per-agent value cannot represent. Trained once over the distribution of objectives, it is applied at deployment with no per-task adaptation.

</details>


### [149] [Low-Interaction-Rank Learning: Unifying Multiplicative Dual-Encoder Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11661)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"低交互秩"函数类作为乘法双编码器网络的统一理论框架，将逼近误差分解为谱截断项和编码器实现项，指出样本复杂度由两个编码器复杂度之和决定（而非乘积），并通过谱衰减准则判定架构适用性。论文还揭示了一个核心可辨识性问题：编码器仅定义到线性规范对称性，归一化是规范固定而白化可将交互模式钉扎到置换和符号级别，从而解释了对比学习维度不可解释的原因并提供了构造性修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法双编码器网络（将一对输入的内积作为输出）已在算子学习、二分匹配、对比视觉-语言模型、检索等多个领域独立发展，但缺乏统一理论来指导基本设计决策：交互模式数量、编码器归一化方式以及何时应避免使用该架构。

Method: 引入低交互秩函数类，其内在复杂度由交互谱度量。在此框架下，逼近误差分解为谱截断项与编码器实现项；从规范对称性出发，证明归一化等价于规范固定，白化可将交互模式固定到置换和符号级别。

Result: 在合成核、算子学习和CLIP模型上的实验验证了理论预测：谱衰减速率符合预期标度，白化能恢复真实模式，独立训练的CLIP模型之间由一个旋转关联，去除该旋转后（通过白化）可暴露可解释的概念轴。

Conclusion: 该框架为乘法双编码器网络提供了统一的理论基础，包括设计准则（基于谱衰减的可用性判据）、归一化的本质理解（规范固定），以及对比学习维度不可解释性的解决方案（白化修复）。

Abstract: A multiplicative dual-encoder network computes a real-valued output for a pair of inputs as the inner product of their separate encodings. This architecture has been developed independently in operator learning, bipartite matching, contrastive vision-language models, retrieval, and other areas, yet no unified theory guides the basic design decisions: how many interaction modes to represent, how to normalize the encoders, and when the architecture should be avoided. We provide such a foundation by introducing the class of functions of low interaction rank, a class whose intrinsic complexity is measured by its interaction spectrum. Within this framework, approximation error decomposes into a spectral truncation term and an encoder-realization term; sample complexity is governed by the sum of the two encoder complexities rather than their product; and a usability criterion based on spectral decay determines when the architecture can succeed. The same framework exposes a central identifiability problem: the encoders are defined only up to a linear gauge symmetry that leaves the learned coordinates arbitrary. We show that normalization is gauge fixing and that whitening pins the interaction modes up to permutation and sign, thereby explaining the uninterpretability of contrastive dimensions and providing a constructive remedy. Experiments on synthetic kernels, operator learning, and CLIP models validate the theoretical predictions: spectral decay rates match the predicted scaling, whitening recovers the true modes, and independently trained CLIP models are related by a single rotation which, after removal by whitening, exposes interpretable concept axes. The code of this paper is provided at https://github.com/RS2002/Mul-Net .

</details>


### [150] [Rubric Dropout: A Simple Way to Mitigate Reward Hacking in Rubric-as-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11669)
*Minglai Yang,Xinyu Guo,Utkarsh Tyagi,Mian Zhang,Razvan Dumitru,Sunjie Hou,Yunzhong He,Daniel Yue Zhang,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于LLM评判标准的强化学习（GRPO）会导致奖励黑客（reward hacking）：训练评判分数持续上升，而更强的黄金评判分数在训练中达到峰值后下降。作者提出Rubric Dropout——每次训练随机丢弃一部分标准子集，防止策略过度优化固定评分标准，在保持域内性能的同时提升了OOD黄金分数并降低了黑客指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分标准（rubric）的强化学习已成为无确定答案任务上对语言模型进行后训练的标准方法，但评分标准只是质量的固定代理，永远无法完全描述真实质量。当策略针对固定评分标准训练足够久，它会学习利用评分标准与真实质量之间的差异（即奖励黑客）。作者希望直接量化这种偏移并寻找缓解方法。

Method: 1) 使用GRPO在医学和科学评分标准上训练Qwen3-8B，同时用训练评判和更强的黄金评判在OOD基准上评分，直接测量两分数的分歧。2) 提出Rubric Dropout：每步随机丢弃评分标准的一个子集后再计算奖励（丢弃子集在rollout组内共享以保持GRPO组内相对优势可比），评估时始终使用完整评分标准。3) 在无丢弃、30%和50%丢弃率两种基准对上比较效果，并扫描丢弃率找到最优区间，同时对比"按标准对训练的有用性重新加权"这一自然替代方案。

Result: 训练评判分数持续上升而黄金评判分数先升后降（HealthBench-Hard下降3分，ResearchQA下降22分），确认是奖励黑客而非评判噪声（固定偏差只会平移黄金曲线）。Rubric Dropout在30%和50%丢弃率下，在所有匹配的检查点均提升OOD黄金分数（HealthBench-Hard +1至+2分，ResearchQA +6至+7分），降低两种黑客指标，且不损失域内性能。30-50%丢弃率是宽泛的最优区间；而按标准实用性重新加权的替代方案比不干预更差。

Conclusion: Rubric Dropout是一种简单有效的防止基于LLM评分标准强化学习中奖励黑客的方法。通过在训练中随机丢弃标准子集，策略无法反复优化同一个评分标准，从而减少对质量代理的攻击。该方法零域内代价、实现简单（一行修复），且在多种设置下一致优于无干预和重新加权方案。作者建议在实践中有意引入随机性来破坏策略对固定分数映射的过度拟合。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning against rubrics, lists of criteria graded by an LLM judge, has become a standard way to post-train language models on tasks with no deterministic answer. The rubric, however, is a fixed proxy for quality, never a complete description of it, and a policy trained against it long enough will learn to exploit the difference. We measure this directly. Training Qwen3-8B with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on medical and science rubrics and grading out-of-distribution (OOD) benchmarks with both the training judge and a stronger gold judge, we find that the two scores diverge during training. The training judge's score keeps climbing while the gold judge's score peaks and then falls, by 3 points on HealthBench-Hard and by 22 points on ResearchQA. A judge with a fixed bias would shift the gold curve by a constant, not send it down while the training score rises, so the divergence is reward hacking, not judge noise. We propose Rubric Dropout, a one-line fix borrowed from neuron dropout. At every step, we randomly drop a subset of the rubric's criteria before computing the reward, so the policy never optimizes the same rubric twice. The dropped subset is shared across each rollout group, so GRPO's group-relative advantages stay comparable, and evaluation always uses the full rubric. Comparing no dropout against dropout at 30% and 50% on both benchmark pairs, dropout raises the OOD gold score at every matched checkpoint (+1 to +2 points on HealthBench-Hard, +6 to +7 points on ResearchQA), lowers the two hacking measures we track, and costs nothing in domain. Sweeping the dropout fraction shows a broad 30-50% sweet spot, while the natural alternative, reweighting criteria by how useful they are to training, performs worse than no intervention at all in our setting.

</details>


### [151] [GCPO: Diagnosing and Constraining Subspace Geometry in Rollout RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11674)
*Kai Yang,Jingwei Xu,Wanyu Wang,Kai-Yuan Guo,Zhenbo Yu,Yi Wang,Yu Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出主子空间重叠度（PSO）作为衡量单个rollout更新与预训练权重主奇异子空间重叠程度的诊断指标，并基于此设计了几何约束策略优化（GCPO）方法，通过硬性双侧正交投影将更新约束在互补子空间中，解决了GRPO训练的不稳定性、跨任务能力下降和响应长度膨胀问题，在Qwen3-8B和GLM4-9B上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等on-policy rollout方法在LLM后训练中广泛使用，但面临训练不稳定、跨任务能力退化、响应长度膨胀等问题。先前研究虽刻画了聚合更新的子空间几何，但逐步骤的几何变化及其与模型性能的关系尚不清楚，这阻碍了对训练稳定性的理解与改进。

Method: 首先提出Principal-Subspace Overlap（PSO），一种维度校正的度量指标，用于衡量单个rollout更新相对于预训练权重主导奇异子空间的重叠程度。观察到平均重叠虽低但瞬态尖峰常先于性能退化出现。基于此提出GCPO（Geometrically Constrained Policy Optimization），通过对更新施加硬性双侧正交投影，将参数更新约束在与主导奇异子空间互补的子空间中，从构造上阻止有害的几何偏离。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和工具使用任务上，基于Qwen3-8B和GLM4-9B的实验中，GCPO一致优于GRPO及近期变体（如DAPO和GSPO），相比基座模型和最强基线分别提升最多27.69和2.37个百分点。此外，GCPO保持了通用能力，消除了响应长度膨胀，并稳定了策略熵。

Conclusion: 本文提供了用于诊断RL后训练稳定性的新视角（PSO指标）以及基于几何约束的设计原则（GCPO），为稳定强化学习后训练提供了理论指导与实践验证。

Abstract: On-policy rollout methods such as GRPO are central to post-training of large language models, yet they frequently suffer from training instabilities, cross-task capability degradation, and response-length inflation. Although prior work has characterized the subspace geometry of aggregate updates, the stepwise variation of this geometry and its relationship to model performance remain unclear. We introduce Principal-Subspace Overlap, a dimension-corrected measure of individual rollout updates relative to the dominant singular subspaces of pretrained weights. Despite low average overlap, transient spikes often precede performance degradation. To address this, we propose GCPO (Geometrically Constrained Policy Optimization), which applies hard bilateral orthogonal projections to constrain updates to the complementary subspaces, preventing such excursions by construction. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and tool-use tasks on Qwen3-8B and GLM4-9B, GCPO consistently outperforms GRPO and recent variants, including DAPO and GSPO, improving over the base models and the strongest baseline by up to 27.69 and 2.37 points, respectively. Furthermore, GCPO preserves general capabilities, eliminates response-length inflation, and stabilizes policy entropy. Our findings provide a new diagnostic lens and a principled design perspective for stable reinforcement learning post-training.

</details>


### [152] [FunnelCausalNet: Funnel-aware Joint Conversion-Revenue Uplift for Multi-tier Coupon Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11675)
*Yu Zhang,Zhihan Wang,Guanlin Chen,Min Jiang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FunnelCausalNet，一种将二分类转化率头与非负条件客单价头耦合成GMV漏斗的增量估计器（u_gmv = u_conv × u_val）。该方法配备边际分裂共形CATE摘要、Bonferroni联合审计带及基于RCT锚定的拉格朗日预算分配器。在Criteo-MT7半合成数据上达到领先基线水平，在工业券RCT日志（约490万样本）中于全部七个相关锚点取得最优平均DeltaROI，并揭示了稀疏二值花费基准上收入导向排序器优于提升曲线代理的机制边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优惠券活动旨在同时提升转化率和收入，但GMV遵循从转化到条件订单价值的确定性漏斗，且存在零膨胀和重尾分布。直接回归GMV难以处理这种数据结构，需要一种能够明确建模漏斗结构的增量估计方法，以更好地指导预算分配和ROI核算。

Method: FunnelCausalNet将二元转化预测头与条件价值预测头通过u_gmv = u_conv × u_val耦合。在显式RCT、支持条件、率差及跨头协方差控制假设下，通过理想化主导阶MSE比较识别漏斗组合可降低逐点方差的机制；使用边际分裂共形CATE汇总并通过Bonferroni联合构成审计带；采用基于RCT锚定估计的拉格朗日预算分配器进行补贴感知的ROI核算。

Result: 在Criteo-MT7上，FunnelCausalNet的均值AUUC_GMV与11个基线中领先的特征交互基线在一个种子标准差以内；消融实验在测试零膨胀区间将GMV效应误差相对直接GMV回归降低18%-48%。在工业酒店券RCT数据（约490万保持样本）上，于10%至60%的七个相关锚点均取得种子平均最优DeltaROI。在稀疏二值花费基准上揭示了收入导向排序器可主导提升曲线代理的机制边界。

Conclusion: FunnelCausalNet通过漏斗结构耦合有效改善了GMV增量估计，在工业规模数据上表现出最优的预算-ROI前沿一致性。作者审慎地将工业结果视为描述性的前沿一致性而非独立性显著，并明确界定了不同数据分布下收入导向排序与提升曲线代理的适用边界。

Abstract: Coupon campaigns seek to lift both conversion and revenue, but gross merchandise value (GMV) follows a deterministic funnel from conversion to conditional order value and is zero-inflated and heavy-tailed. We propose FunnelCausalNet, an uplift estimator coupling a binary conversion head with a nonnegative conditional-value head through $μ_{\mathrm{gmv}}=μ_{\mathrm{conv}}μ_{\mathrm{val}}$. Under explicit RCT, support, rate-gap, and cross-head covariance-control assumptions, an idealized leading-order MSE comparison identifies a regime in which funnel composition can reduce pointwise variance; this is a heuristic, not a guarantee for the shared-representation neural model. The estimator is paired with marginal split-conformal CATE summaries, combined through a Bonferroni union as audit bands, and a Lagrangian budgeted allocator using RCT-anchored estimates for subsidy-aware ROI accounting. On semi-synthetic multi-tier Criteo-MT7, FunnelCausalNet's mean AUUC_GMV is within one seed standard deviation of the leading feature-interaction baseline among eleven baselines, while a controlled ablation reduces GMV effect error versus direct GMV regression by 18--48% across tested zero-inflation regimes. On de-identified industrial Hotel-Coupon RCT logs with about 4.9 million hold-out exposure records per seed, expected-outcome evaluation sweeps full LP frontiers; FunnelCausalNet has the best seed-averaged mean DeltaROI at all seven correlated anchors from 10% to 60%, which we treat as descriptive frontier consistency rather than independent significance. On sparse binary-spend public benchmarks, revenue-focused rankers can dominate uplift-curve proxies, defining an explicit regime boundary.

</details>


### [153] [LEMUR: Latent Entropy-aware Multimodal Unlearning via Visual-anchored Reasoning Redirection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11691)
*Xinhao Zhong,Yuxia Qiao,Junhao Li,Hao Fang,Yi Sun,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习（RL）后训练赋予多模态大推理模型（MLRMs）的探索性思维链能力引入了一种独特的隐私漏洞：即使敏感事实已从最终答案中被成功遗忘，模型仍可能在推理过程中复现该信息。基于RL诱导的探索在敏感内容上留下独特的token级熵特征这一观察，作者提出LEMUR——一个完全无需训练、推理时的遗忘框架，通过熵动力学作为控制信号识别敏感推理的起始点和消毒终止点，并在该区间通过熵调制的视觉锚点隐式注入来重定向推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的遗忘（unlearning）方法仅关注最终答案的隐私保护，未能应对RL训练的推理模型在思考链（CoT）中泄露敏感信息的风险。随着RL驱动的多模态大推理模型（MLRMs）被广泛部署，其推理轨迹中的隐私泄露构成了一个全新且未被解决的安全威胁。

Method: LEMUR框架包含以下核心组件：(1) 利用RL诱导的熵动力学作为控制信号，检测敏感推理的起始与结束；(2) 在检测到敏感区间时，通过熵调制的视觉锚点隐式注入（entropy-modulated visual-anchor latent injection）重定向推理路径；(3) 用基于概率加权的安全嵌入替换已承诺的敏感token，重新扎根于输入图像，实现无需训练的推理时遗忘。

Result: 在多种多模态大推理模型上，LEMUR在抑制推理轨迹泄露和答案泄露方面均一致优于现有遗忘方法，同时更好地保留了非敏感内容的效用和输出流畅性。

Conclusion: RL诱导的熵动力学为隐私泄露提供了独特的检测信号，利用该信号可以实现对具备推理能力的多模态模型进行有效的、无需训练的推理时遗忘，为推理模型的隐私保护开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training equips multimodal large reasoning models (MLRMs) with exploratory chains of thought (CoT), substantially improving visual reasoning. However, we find that this capability introduces a distinct privacy vulnerability: even when a sensitive fact is successfully unlearned from the final answer, the model may still reproduce it in its reasoning trace. This leakage is substantially more pronounced in natively RL-trained MLRMs than in their non -reasoning base models, revealing a privacy risk that existing unlearning methods are not designed to address. We show that RL-induced exploration leaves sensitive content with a distinctive token-level entropy signature that is largely absent from base models. Based on this observation, we propose LEMUR, a fully training-free, inference-time unlearning framework for natively RL-trained multimodal models. LEMUR uses entropy dynamics as a control signal to identify when sensitive reasoning begins and when sanitization should stop. During this interval, it redirects the reasoning trajectory through entropy-modulated visual-anchor latent injection, replacing committed tokens with sanitized, probability-weighted embeddings re-grounded in the input image. Across diverse MLRMs, LEMUR consistently outperforms existing unlearning met hods in suppressing both reasoning-trace and answer leakage, while better preserving non-sensitive utility and output fluency. These results demonstrate that RL-induced entropy dynamics provide a distinctive signal for privacy leakage and that exploiting this signal enables effective training-free unlearning for reasoning-capable multimodal models.

</details>


### [154] [REOPD: Reliability-Adaptive Reward Extrapolation for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11698)
*Yang Sun,Lichao Ma,Houyuan Qin,Yuxin Liu,Hanyang Lu,Yao Zhu,Pinlong Cai,Guohang Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REOPD框架，为在线策略蒸馏（OPD）提供可靠性自适应的奖励外推机制，通过token级兼容性权重和batch级自适应预算，实现对教师参考方向的选择性外推，避免全局单一系数导致的奖励黑客问题，且无需额外验证器、奖励模型或额外rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励外推方法（如ExOPD）对每个token使用全局单一系数λ，可能导致学生过度拟合隐式奖励中的极端峰值，引发奖励黑客和不稳定训练；且最优λ跨域变化大，需要昂贵的超参数搜索。

Method: REOPD结合token级兼容性权重与batch级自适应预算，构建token级系数λ_{b,t}=1+γ_b q_t，在保持教师对齐的同时沿可靠的教师参考方向进行选择性外推。该方法无需验证器、奖励模型、价值模型或额外rollout，仅依赖标准OPD流程。

Result: REOPD在单教师数学任务和多教师设置的两种域上均优于G-OPD，在单教师代码任务上与G-OPD持平，展示了跨域和不同教师配置下的细粒度可靠性自适应能力。

Conclusion: REOPD通过细粒度的token级可靠性自适应，在不同域和教师配置下均能有效提升OPD的性能，同时避免了全局系数带来的奖励黑客问题。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories under dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Reward-extrapolation methods such as ExOPD amplify the teacher-reference log-likelihood ratio to move beyond direct imitation, but apply a single global coefficient $λ$ to every token. This can drive the student to fit extreme peaks in the implicit reward, causing reward hacking and unstable training, and the optimal $λ$ varies across domains, requiring costly sweeps. We propose REOPD, a reliability-adaptive reward extrapolation framework for OPD. REOPD combines a token-level compatibility weight with a batch-level adaptive budget, yielding a token-wise coefficient $λ_{b,t}=1+γ_b q_t$ that preserves teacher alignment while selectively extrapolating along reliable teacher-reference directions. It requires no verifier, reward model, value model, or extra rollout beyond standard OPD. REOPD outperforms G-OPD on single-teacher mathematics and on both domains in the multi-teacher setting, while matching G-OPD on single-teacher code, demonstrating effective fine-grained reliability adaptation across domains and teacher configurations.

</details>


### [155] [Consolidator: Learning Persistent Routed Memory Across Context Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11701)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究短期记忆（STM）持久化到长期记忆（LTM）后，保留的状态是否不仅能被读取，还能影响后续记忆访问。在Phasor Memory Network（PMNet）中使用共享的slot-local算子Consolidator将路由后的STM转换并累积到LTM中（不重放源token），清空KV cache和STM后，LTM既可作为可检索内容，也能反馈给层次路由器以调节后续输入访问的显式记忆槽位。在分段模10映射任务上，仅训练12.35K个Consolidator参数（占0.041%），直接LTM路由使更新映射的召回率从44.38%提升至87.02%（+42.64个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆持久化机制只关注状态跨上下文边界的保留，但持久化本身并不能保证保留的状态能影响后续的记忆访问。作者希望区分"持久化"与"调节后续访问"这两个概念，验证经过整合（consolidation）的长期记忆是否既能作为可检索的内容，又能作为条件化后续槽位选择的状态。

Method: 在Phasor Memory Network中引入Consolidator（共享slot-local算子），将路由后的短期记忆（STM）转换后累积进长期记忆（LTM），不重放源token。每次整合后清空KV cache和STM，保留的LTM可被读取，同时被馈送到层次路由器中，从而调节后续输入访问的显式记忆槽位。在二段式modulo-10映射任务上评估，第二段更新同一内存地址的映射，第二次整合和重置后用held-out查询从LTM恢复更新后的映射。骨架和记忆接口冻结，仅训练12.35K Consolidator参数。

Result: 五对配对实验（同一STM预训练checkpoint出发）显示，直接LTM路由将更新映射的召回率从44.38±1.94%提升至87.02±1.76%（+42.64±1.10个百分点），而两类条件下即时STM召回率均为89.90%。学习式整合（learned consolidation）相比强制恒等累积（forced identity accumulation）在无路由时提升21.40±1.91个百分点，有路由时提升68.70±1.76个百分点。

Conclusion: 在该任务上，整合后的LTM既作为可检索的内容，也作为塑造后续槽位选择的访问状态（access state）。学习式整合显著优于恒等累积，且LTM的路由功能是提升更新映射召回率的关键机制。

Abstract: Copying short-term memory (STM) into a slower store can preserve state across a context boundary, but persistence alone does not ensure that the retained state influences subsequent memory access. We test this distinction in a Phasor Memory Network (PMNet) using Consolidator, a shared slot-local operator that transforms routed STM before accumulating it into long-term memory (LTM), without replaying the source tokens. After each consolidation, the KV cache and STM are cleared. The retained LTM can still be read and is also fed into the hierarchical router, thereby conditioning which explicit-memory slots subsequent inputs access. We evaluate this mechanism on a two-segment modulo-10 mapping task in which the second segment updates the mapping at the same memory address. Following a second consolidation and reset, a held-out query must recover the updated mapping from LTM. The backbone and memory interface are frozen, leaving only 12.35K Consolidator parameters trainable (0.041\% of a 29.95M model). Across five paired runs from the same STM-pretraining checkpoint, direct LTM routing raises updated-mapping recall from $44.38\pm1.94\%$ to $87.02\pm1.76\%$ ($+42.64\pm1.10$ percentage points), while immediate STM recall remains 89.90\% in both conditions; both train separate Consolidators and retain the same LTM read paths. Learned consolidation outperforms forced identity accumulation by $21.40\pm1.91$ percentage points without routing and $68.70\pm1.76$ with routing. Thus, on this task, consolidated LTM serves as both retrievable content and an access state that shapes subsequent slot selection.

</details>


### [156] [Robust and Efficient Noisy-Label Time-Series Classification via Dynamic Time Warping Based Granular Ball Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11704)
*Ziqiang Li,Yun Liu,Gouhei Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DTW-GBC（基于动态时间规整的粒度球计算）方法，将时间相似性样本组织成粒度球并在粒级进行分类，在四个基准数据集上验证了其对标签噪声的鲁棒性和推理效率的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于DTW的最近邻分类器在时间序列分类中有效，但对训练样本的误标签敏感，且推理时需要大量DTW计算，效率低下。

Method: 提出DTW-GBC框架，将时间上相似的训练样本组织成粒度球，在粒度层面进行分类；并设计了两种粒度球构建策略作为DTW-GBC的变体。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上加入对称标签噪声的实验表明，两种DTW-GBC变体通常能缓解标签噪声造成的性能下降，同时推理时所需比较次数远少于DTW-based 1-NN。

Conclusion: DTW-GBC在分类鲁棒性和推理效率之间提供了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based Nearest-Neighbor (NN) classifiers are effective for time-series classification but are vulnerable to mislabeled training samples and require numerous DTW computations during inference. We propose DTW-based Granular Ball Computing (DTW-GBC), which organizes temporally similar training samples into granular balls and performs classification at the granule level. We further develop two granular-ball construction strategies for DTW-GBC. Experiments on four benchmark datasets with symmetric label noise show that the two DTW-GBC variants generally mitigate the performance degradation caused by label noise while requiring substantially fewer comparisons than DTW-based 1-NN during inference. These findings suggest that DTW-GBC provides a favorable balance between classification robustness and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [157] [High-dimensional Multi-objective Bayesian Optimization with Learned Variable Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11713)
*Hongyan Wang,Jiayu Huang,Haotian Zheng,Xin Gao,Chi Ding,Ying Liu,Xia Wang,Qing Xu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ViaMOBO，一种基于决策变量交互分析的多目标贝叶斯优化框架，通过分析变量间的独立/依赖关系来决定决策空间是否可分，并在分解的子空间中进行局部贝叶斯优化，从而解决高维昂贵多目标优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数MOBO方法受限于低维决策空间，因为其采样复杂度随维度呈指数增长，难以应对高维昂贵黑箱多目标优化问题。

Method: ViaMOBO利用变量交互分析模型判断决策空间是否可以完全或部分分解，然后分别在分解后的子空间中进行局部贝叶斯优化。该变量分析模型能够在无需强假设的前提下，基于决策变量之间潜在独立或相互依赖的关系，推导出黑箱目标函数是可分离、部分可分离或不可分离的。

Result: 在合成基准和真实世界基准上的实验表明，ViaMOBO在逼近高维昂贵多目标问题的Pareto前沿方面优于其他相关MOBO基线方法。

Conclusion: ViaMOBO通过变量交互分析实现决策空间的分解，有效克服了高维MOBO的指数级采样复杂度问题，为高维昂贵多目标优化提供了一种通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) is effective in identifying the Pareto fronts for expensive black-box problems. However, most current MOBO approaches are limited to low-dimensional decision space due to its exponential sampling complexity. This paper presents decision variable interaction analysis-based MOBO, ViaMOBO, a generic framework for expensive multi-objective problems with high-dimensional decision space. The key idea of ViaMOBO is that it utilizes a variable interaction analysis model to determine whether the decision space can be completely or partially divided, and then performs local Bayesian optimization in the divided decision subspaces. Through the variable analysis model, it can be derived whether the objectives in black-box problems are separable, partially separable, or non-separable based on the potential independent or interdependent relationships among decision variables without any strong assumptions. We compare ViaMOBO with the state-of-the-art MOBO methods on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks. The experimental results demonstrate that ViaMOBO outperforms other related MOBO baselines in approximating the Pareto front of high-dimensional expensive multi-objective problems.

</details>


### [158] [Chain-of-Thought Shows the Path to a Tree: Realizing Branching Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11716)
*Debanjan Dutta,Anish Chakrabarty,Swagatam Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Chain of Thought（CoT）与电路复杂度类之间已有理论刻画但缺乏具体深度受限Transformer构造的空白，给出了分支复杂度（branching complexity）的显式构造：用至多两层的硬注意力解码器实现深度优先搜索（DFS）和Dijkstra算法（后者涵盖广度优先搜索），并以此为共享计算基座，分别用四层解码器在2n-1步内计算n顶点树的Strahler数，用三层解码器在n-1步内计算其宽度。通过DFS构造实现的DFS与有序树到Dyck路径的经典双射，作者还给出了基于路径表示的两种测度的独立构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有工作将CoT步骤数与电路复杂度类建立联系（如NC¹等），但这主要停留在理论刻画层面，缺乏具体、显式的深度受限Transformer构造，也没有明确实现这些刻画所预设的遍历过程（traversal procedures）。本文旨在填补这一空白，为分支复杂度提供具体的深度有界Transformer实例。

Method: 作者设计了两种至多两层的唯一硬注意力（unique hard-attention）解码器：一个实现DFS遍历，另一个实现Dijkstra算法（可退化涵盖BFS）。这些构造不使用层归一化或位置编码。基于这两个"共享计算基座"，作者通过复用DFS解码器（加两层，共四层）在2n-1步内计算Strahler数，复用Dijkstra解码器（加一层，共三层）在n-1步内计算树宽度。此外，利用DFS遍历自然发射出有序树对应的Dyck路径，基于该路径表示给出了两种测度的独立构造。

Result: 给出了DFS和Dijkstra算法的显式两阶段硬注意力解码器构造；在此基础上实现了任意n叉树的Strahler数计算（2n-1步、四层）和宽度计算（n-1步、三层）。由于二叉项树的Strahler数计算是NC¹完全的，且构造不依赖层归一化或位置编码，这为CoT层次结构中的线性步区（linear-step regime）提供了一个非平凡的证据。DFS构造还实现了有序树与Dyck路径的经典双射，由此给出路径表示上的独立构造。

Conclusion: 作者证明了分支复杂度的核心度量（Strahler数和宽度）可以通过常数深度、线性步数的CoT Transformer高效计算，填补了理论复杂度刻画与具体构造之间的鸿沟。这些构造同时展现了DFS/Dijkstra解码器作为通用计算基座的价值，为理解CoT层次中线性步集的表达能力提供了坚实的具体实例。

Abstract: Chain of Thought (CoT) lifts the expressive ceiling of bounded-depth Transformers, with characterizations tying the number of CoT steps to circuit complexity classes. What remains largely missing are concrete instantiations with explicit, depth-bounded constructions, and the traversal procedures such characterizations presuppose. We close this gap for branching complexity. We give CoT realizations of depth-first search (DFS) and of Dijkstra algorithm, the latter subsuming breadth-first search, by unique hard-attention decoders of at most two layers, and use them as a shared computational substrate: reusing the DFS decoder yields the Strahler number of an $n$-vertex tree in $2n-1$ steps with four layers, and reusing the Dijkstra decoder yields its width in $n-1$ steps with three. Since computing the Strahler number of a binary tree given as a term is \textsf{NC\textsuperscript{1}}-complete, and our constructions handle arbitrary $n$-ary trees without layer normalization or positional encodings, this is a non-trivial witness for the linear-step regime of the CoT hierarchy. Exploiting the classical bijection between ordered trees and Dyck paths, itself realized by our DFS construction, which emits the path as it traverses, we give independent constructions for both measures on the path representation.

</details>


### [159] [Epiplexity Guided Data Selection and Generation for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11746)
*Ellen Su,Andres Potapczynski,Shikai Qiu,Edward Hughes,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将Epiplexity（一种衡量计算受限学习者能从数据中提取的结构信息量的指标）作为在线训练信号，用于数据选择和合成数据生成。实验表明，高Epiplexity数据能提升零样本和微调任务的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代系统需要在训练时未指定的任务上进行迁移，但尚不清楚哪些数据能促进这种未预见场景下的泛化。作者假设富含结构信息的数据包含可复用的共享电路和子程序，能在更广泛的下游设置中被利用。Epiplexity为此提供了理论机制，但尚未被操作化为实际的训练信号。

Method: 1) 数据选择：对自然数据域的训练损失曲线拟合缩放定律，预测Epiplexity增益随训练token数量的变化，进而自适应地确定训练中各域的采样权重。2) 合成数据生成：将生成器的奖励定义为学习者对先前生成数据缓冲区的Epiplexity变化，使用REINFORCE策略梯度引导生成器走向最大化Epiplexity的分布。

Result: 在两种方法下，更高的Epiplexity均预测了更好的下游零样本和微调任务表现，验证了结构信息丰富的数据能产生跨域迁移的表示。

Conclusion: 该研究支持了"结构信息丰富的数据促进跨域泛化"的假设，并将理论概念Epiplexity成功转化为实用的数据选择与合成工具，为数据驱动的泛化提供了可操作的训练信号。

Abstract: Modern systems are increasingly expected to transfer across tasks not specified during training. What data facilitates generalization in these new, unanticipated settings? One hypothesis is that data with more structural information could contain shared circuits and subprograms that could be recycled in a wider array of downstream settings. Epiplexity, a recently proposed measure of the structural information a compute-bounded learner can extract from data, provides a mechanism to reason about this relationship. In this paper, we show how to operationalize epiplexity as an online training signal for data selection and synthetic data generation. For selection, we fit scaling laws to the training loss curves of natural data domains to predict the expected epiplexity gain as a function of training tokens, and use this signal to adaptively determine the sampling weights over domains during training. For synthetic data generation, we define a generator's reward as the change in learner epiplexity over a buffer of previously generated data and use REINFORCE policy gradients to guide the generator toward an epiplexity-maximizing distribution. In both cases, higher epiplexity predicts improved downstream performance on zero-shot and fine-tuning based tasks, supporting the hypothesis that data rich in structural information yield representations that transfer across domains.

</details>


### [160] [TradingMoE: Routing the Right Experts in Evolving Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11785)
*Chang Zhou,Xingtong Yu,Minbin Huang,Zhennan Wu,Yuan Fang,Hong Cheng,Xinming Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TradingMoE提出了一种面向交易的稀疏混合专家（MoE）框架，通过Query-Key路由器将每个token在当前市场情境下所需的专长表示为低维查询并与可学习专家键匹配，并引入稀疏专家选择更新机制，使路由器能随市场条件变化动态调整专家选择，在股票和加密货币市场上分别比最优基线提升了30.89%和30.7%的累计收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的交易系统存在两个核心问题：一是依赖人工定义的外部专家或采用未能直接评估各专家对交易决策贡献的传统MoE路由器；二是路由器缺乏直接信号来感知在市场条件变化时原本不活跃的专家是否变得更加适用。研究发现原生路由器评分难以反映专家对交易决策的实际改善程度，经常遗漏更优的专家选项。

Method: TradingMoE采用冻结的密集LLM并附加轻量级残差专家。其核心由两部分组成：1）Query-Key路由器，将当前市场上下文下每个token所需的专长压缩为低维查询向量，并与可学习的专家键进行匹配；2）稀疏专家选择更新机制，在训练过程中采样少量不活跃专家，评估其是否应替换当前Top-k路由中最弱的专家，从而在保持稀疏计算的同时使路由器能够随市场变化更新专家选择。

Result: 在股票和加密货币市场上与22个基线方法对比，TradingMoE分别将累计收益提升了30.89%和30.7%。滚动纸面交易实验进一步验证了其优势在仅前向部署下依然保持。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了一个关键发现：token级别的专家有用性呈现紧凑的低维结构，这为设计低维查询路由提供了理论依据。TradingMoE通过结合低维查询-键路由和稀疏专家选择更新机制，有效解决了传统MoE路由器无法动态适应市场变化的问题，在金融交易场景中取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for financial analysis and trading, but direct trading remains challenging because the predictive capabilities required can vary across assets, decision fields, and market conditions. Existing LLM-based trading systems either coordinate human-defined external experts or adopt conventional internal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers that do not directly evaluate how individual experts contribute to trading decisions. Moreover, these routers receive no direct signal indicating when an inactive expert has become more suitable as market conditions change. We find that native router scores poorly reflect how much individual experts improve trading decisions, frequently leaving better alternatives unselected. We further reveal that token-specific expert usefulness exhibits a compact low-dimensional structure. Based on these findings, we propose TradingMoE, a trading-oriented sparse MoE that augments a frozen dense LLM with lightweight residual experts. We introduce a Query-Key router that represents the expertise required by each token under the current market context as a low-dimensional query and matches it with learnable expert keys. We further propose a sparse expert selection update mechanism that samples a few inactive experts during training and estimates whether they should replace the weakest expert in the current Top-k route. This mechanism enables the router to update expert selection as market conditions change while preserving sparse computation. Experiments against 22 baselines on stock and cryptocurrency markets show that TradingMoE improves cumulative return over the best-performing baselines by 30.89% and 30.7%, respectively. Rolling paper-trading experiments further demonstrate that its advantage persists under forward-only deployment.

</details>


### [161] [High-Order Liquid Evidence Encoding for Gradual GNSS Spoofing Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11790)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Fatima Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种因果高阶液态证据框架用于GNSS欺骗检测，通过构建物理引导的GNSS-运动不一致残差及其一阶、二阶离散变化作为多流证据，结合自适应液态编码器和层次化耦合来检测连续微妙的GNSS欺骗攻击，在真实世界AV-GPS数据集上取得最高F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续且微妙的GNSS欺骗攻击难以检测，因为单个GNSS观测量看似合理，但GNSS隐含位移与车载运动之间的不一致会逐渐累积。现有方法依赖静态车辆行为特征或单一残差信号，未显式建模这种演化过程。

Method: 1) 构建物理引导的GNSS-运动不一致残差，比较GNSS隐含位移与车载运动推导位移；2) 分别形成残差水平及其一阶、二阶离散变化的多条证据流，并依据证据阶数选择相关上下文线索；3) 每条流由独立的自适应液态编码器处理，时间状态被层次化耦合，仅使用当前和过去的观测在窗口端点预测欺骗。

Result: 在真实世界AV-GPS数据集的三个子集上，该方法在Dataset 1和Dataset 3上达到最高F1分数，分别为0.9535和0.9777；在Dataset 3上能在四个采样步内检测出两个标注的正常-攻击转换。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模GNSS欺骗信号中不一致性的高阶时序演化，该框架能有效检测连续微妙的GNSS欺骗攻击，优于现有时间模型，且代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Accurate Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-based localization is essential for safe and reliable autonomous driving. However, spoofing attacks can manipulate vehicle position estimates. Continuous and subtle attacks are particularly difficult to detect because individual GNSS observations may remain plausible while the inconsistency between GNSS-implied displacement and onboard vehicle motion gradually increases. Existing methods often rely on static vehicle-behavior features or a single residual signal and do not explicitly model this evolution. To address this problem, we propose a causal high-order liquid evidence framework for GNSS spoofing detection. The method first constructs a physics-guided GNSS--motion inconsistency residual by comparing GNSS-implied displacement with onboard-motion-derived displacement. It then forms separate evidence streams for the residual level and its first- and second-order discrete variations, with relevant contextual cues selected according to the evidence order. Each stream is processed by a separate adaptive liquid encoder, and the resulting temporal states are hierarchically coupled to predict spoofing at the window endpoint using only current and past observations. Experiments on three subsets of the real-world AV-GPS dataset show that the proposed method achieves the highest F1-scores among the evaluated temporal models on Dataset~1 and Dataset~3, reaching 0.9535 and 0.9777, respectively. On Dataset~3, it detects both labeled normal-to-attack transitions within four sampling steps. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/GNSS_Spoofing.git.

</details>


### [162] [Orientation, not magnitude: the causal structure of task-vector interference in merged language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11797)
*Chencheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过阶乘账本精确追踪和直接干预合并LLM的逐层交叉项，发现"幅度"不足以作为合并失败的诊断轴，交叉项的主要来源是现有交叉项的前向传播放大（占比约65-70%），且该方向是前向传播的吸引子、具有因果承载作用；指令包装器通过模板固定的主效应掩盖交叉项而非缩小它；46个预测全部预先注册。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并中的任务算术（task arithmetic）在模型规模增大或任务冲突时失效，已有诊断主要归因于幅度性指标（如逐层表征偏差、跨任务线性偏离、参数重叠），但这些指标缺乏精确的因果验证。作者希望通过精确分解和直接干预交叉项，检验"幅度"是否足以解释合并失败，并揭示真正的因果机制。

Method: 采用阶乘账本（factorial ledger）对合并LLM的逐层交叉项进行精确分解；直接对交叉项进行擦除干预，测试其在前向传播中的重建与传播行为；通过六种起始位移的盆地测试（basin test）验证携带方向是否为吸引子；使用剂量依赖测试和错方向对照进行因果验证；设计指令包装器（instruction wrapper）与长度匹配对照测试机制可迁移性；全部46个预测预先注册并在数据产生前冻结。

Result: 交叉项的逐层通量主要由已有交叉项的放大传输主导（两个模型族中约占65-70%，每后一层增益>1）；擦除交叉项后会被传播重建至99%的范数（cosine 0.99），除非靠近输出层操作；携带方向是前向传播的吸引子，沿着该方向擦除可剂量依赖地移除表达的干扰并在完全擦除时饱和，而等范数错误方向对照失败或适得其反；指令包装器可门控该效应（相同擦除下相对干扰减少13倍差异），机制被包装器内部的模板固定主效应淹没而非缩小；naive bfloat16生成中±15%的"普适性"实为量化粗糙度；局部交叉项生成差异≤1.9倍的任务对，在可因果移除干扰上相差14x-337x。

Conclusion: 幅度最多只是粗略的相关指标，而非合并失败的有效诊断轴；真正的因果机制是交叉项的方向性传播与吸引子行为。这改变了模型合并诊断的理论基础，提示应关注方向因果而非幅度相关。

Abstract: Model merging by task arithmetic works until it doesn't, and the field diagnoses why with magnitudes: layerwise representation bias, deviations from cross-task linearity, parameter overlap. Tracking the exact layerwise cross-term of merged LLMs through a factorial ledger and intervening on it directly, we find magnitude insufficient - and inconsistent across model families - as a diagnostic axis. An exact decomposition of the layerwise flux shows it is dominated by amplifying transport of the existing cross-term (~65-70% in both families, gain >1 per late block), and erasing the term is undone by propagation - rebuilt to 99% of its norm at cosine 0.99 - unless applied near the output; a basin test with six starting displacements establishes the carried direction as an attractor of the forward pass. That direction is causally load-bearing: erasure along it removes expressed interference dose-dependently and saturates at exact erasure, while norm-matched wrong-direction controls fail or backfire. Instruction wrappers gate the effect: the same erasure finds 13x less relative interference to remove under a wrapper that internally amplifies the cross-term, because the wrapper drowns the interaction in a template-pinned main effect rather than shrinking it - a structure that replicates across further instruction templates but not under a length-matched control. Magnitude, by contrast, is at best a coarse correlate, and the striking +-15% "universality" of naive bfloat16 generation turns out to be quantization roughness. Task pairs whose local cross-term generation differs by at most 1.9x differ by 14x-337x in causally removable interference. All 46 predictions were preregistered and frozen before their data; falsifications, including of our own headline expectations and of behavioral recovery under a validated continuous endpoint, are reported as such.

</details>


### [163] [JAPE: Joint Anomaly Prediction and Intrinsic Explanation in Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11801)
*Yian Wei,Yuanyuan Yao,Lu Chen,Xiangmin Zhou,Tianyi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JAPE框架，将多变量时间序列异常预测从数值偏差建模提升到依赖结构建模，同时实现点级告警和原生变量级解释，在五个真实基准上F1和AUC-PR分别提升19.7%和41.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常预测方法主要将异常建模为未来数值的偏差，忽视弱异常前兆引起的依赖结构变化，且无法提供与告警同时的原生变量级解释。

Method: JAPE包含三大核心组件：(i) 解耦时空表示(DSTR)主干，解耦时间与空间建模，通过可学习滞后聚合捕获滞时依赖；(ii) 双视图告警机制，融合数值预测与演化依赖图进行逐点异常预测；(iii) 原生预测解释(NPE)，直接复用预测依赖图按结构偏差对变量排序，无需额外模型或训练。

Result: 在五个真实基准和三个预测尺度上，JAPE平均F1提升19.7%，AUC-PR提升41.3%，可解释性MRR提升26.6%。

Conclusion: JAPE通过将异常预测从数值偏差建模转向依赖结构建模，能够更早感知结构前兆并原生提供变量级解释，全面超越现有方法。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series anomaly prediction aims to identify whether and when anomalies will occur over a future horizon from historical observations. Existing methods primarily characterize anomalies as deviations in future numerical values, which may overlook subtle dependency changes induced by weak anomaly precursors and provide no native variable-level explanation together with the alert. To bridge these gaps, we propose JAPE, a Joint Anomaly Prediction and Explanation framework that lifts anomaly prediction from numerical-deviation modeling to dependency-structure modeling. JAPE is the first anomaly prediction framework to explicitly model evolving dependency structures for both point-wise alerting and native variable-level explanation. Specifically, JAPE (i) proposes a Decoupled Spatio-Temporal Representation (DSTR) backbone that decouples temporal and spatial modeling and captures lag-aware dependencies via learnable lag aggregation, thereby perceiving structural precursors before numerical deviations emerge; (ii) designs a dual-view alerting mechanism that fuses numerical forecasts with evolving dependency graphs for point-wise anomaly prediction, capturing structural evidence even under subtle numerical deviations; and (iii) presents Native Predictive Explanation (NPE), which directly reuses the predicted dependency graphs to rank variables by structural deviations without additional models or training. Extensive experiments on five real-world benchmarks across three prediction horizons demonstrate that JAPE improves average F1 and AUC-PR by 19.7% and 41.3%, respectively, while improving explainability with 26.6% gain in MRR.

</details>


### [164] [Learning with Bilevel-Minimax Optimization for Efficient and Reliable Transfer Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11815)
*Yaohua Liu,Yifan Guo,Jiaxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BMAT，一种从双层最小最大（bilevel-minimax）视角重新审视迁移攻击的方法。该框架将初始化与扰动的依赖关系建模为双层优化，并通过内层极小极大问题增强代理模型的鲁棒性以实现跨架构泛化。算法上提出了集成自底向上的求解器，结合Soft Weight Modulator和Implicit Gradient Approximator实现初始化、代理模型适应与扰动优化的三元耦合。实验表明BMAT在分类和分割基准上超越10余个强基线、30余个受害模型，并在mIoU上实现高达2倍改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迁移的黑盒攻击研究主要聚焦于扰动生成，而忽略了初始化、代理模型适应与梯度动力学三者之间的耦合关系对迁移性的根本影响，缺乏统一的理论框架来刻画这一复杂交互。

Method: 从bilevel-minimax视角形式化攻击问题：外层双层优化刻画初始化与扰动之间的依赖关系，内层minimax问题增强代理模型鲁棒性以促进跨架构泛化。算法上提出集成的自底向上求解器，包含Soft Weight Modulator（软权重调节器）和Implicit Gradient Approximator（隐式梯度逼近器），实现初始化、代理适应与扰动优化的三元耦合，并提供该框架优化动态的理论分析。

Result: 在分类和分割基准上，BMAT在超过30个受害模型上超越了10余个强基线，同时提升了架构内与跨架构迁移效果，分割任务上mIoU取得最高2倍的改进。

Conclusion: 通过将迁移攻击重新建模为bilevel-minimax优化问题，BMAT揭示了初始化、代理模型适应与扰动优化之间的耦合机制，为提升迁移攻击的跨架构泛化能力提供了新的理论视角与高效算法框架。

Abstract: Transfer-based adversarial attacks craft adversarial examples using surrogate models to mislead black-box victim models. Beyond perturbation generation, transferability is fundamentally governed by the coupling of initialization, surrogate adaptation, and gradient dynamics. We revisit this challenge from a bilevel-minimax perspective and propose BMAT (Bilevel-Minimax Adversarial Transfer). The bilevel formulation captures the dependency between initialization and perturbation, while the inner minimax problem promotes surrogate robustness for cross-architecture generalization. Algorithmically, we develop an integrated bottom-up solver that combines a Soft Weight Modulator and an Implicit Gradient Approximator to enable ternary coupling among initialization, surrogate adaptation, and perturbation optimization. We further provide theoretical insights into the optimization dynamics of the proposed bilevel-minimax framework. Extensive experiments on classification and segmentation benchmarks show that BMAT outperforms more than 10 strong baselines across more than 30 victim models, improving both intra- and cross-architecture transfer and yielding up to a 2x reduction in mIoU. Code is available at https://github.com/callous-youth/BMAT.

</details>


### [165] [Towards Understanding On-Policy Distillation through the Lens of Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11829)
*Xinmu Ge,Zizhuo Zhang,Yu Huang,Jianing Zhu,Lin Yuan,Wanli Gu,Weichang Wu,Weiran Huang,Xiaolu Zhang,Bo Han,Jun Zhou,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过测试时扩展（test-time scaling）的视角重新审视在线蒸馏（OPD）在LLM推理中的作用，发现OPD主要提升采样效率而非真正扩展学生的推理能力边界，表现为avg@K持续优于基础模型但pass@K优势随K增大而消退，并呈现出"虚幻蒸馏"的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为在线蒸馏能使学生模型从更强的教师模型中蒸馏知识，从而扩展其推理能力。本文旨在通过系统地改变采样预算K并评估pass@K和avg@K，检验这一常见观点是否成立，从而更准确地理解OPD的本质作用。

Method: 通过控制不同的OPD变体，变化采样预算K，使用pass@K和avg@K两个指标进行评估；追踪OPD训练过程中pass@K的动态变化；采用pass@1024作为判据进行问题级别的可解性（solvability）分析，对比OPD前后问题从可解变为不可解与从不可解变为可解的不对称性。

Result: 实验发现：(1) OPD训练模型在avg@K上维持各采样预算下的优势；(2) pass@K优势随着K增大逐渐转移回预OPD基础模型；(3) OPD训练过程中pass@K动态显示性能向小K偏移，以牺牲大K能力边界为代价；(4) OPD导致更多原本可解的问题变得不可解，而非相反，呈现不对称性。

Conclusion: 作者提出OPD应被视为"虚幻蒸馏"（illusory distillation）：其表面上的性能提升主要来自采样效率的改善，而非从教师模型真正获取了新的推理能力。从能力扩展的角度来看，OPD并未显著扩大学生的推理能力边界。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as a promising post-training technique for enhancing LLM reasoning. It is commonly believed to enable the student model to distill knowledge from a stronger teacher model, thereby expanding capabilities beyond the pre-OPD base model. In this study, we examine this view through the lens of test-time scaling by varying the sampling budget K and evaluating performance with pass@K and avg@K. Specifically, across several OPD variants, we observe that OPD-trained models maintain superior avg@K performance across sampling budgets, while the advantage in pass@K gradually shifts to the pre-OPD base models as K increases. These results suggest that OPD primarily improves sampling efficiency rather than consistently expanding the student's reasoning capability boundary. The pass@K dynamics throughout OPD training further reveal a progressive shift toward stronger small-K performance at the expense of the large-K capability boundary. Furthermore, a problem-level solvability analysis using pass@1024 as the criterion reveals an asymmetry: OPD causes more previously solvable problems to become unsolvable than previously unsolvable problems to become solvable. Together, these findings suggest that, from the perspective of capability expansion, OPD behaves more like an "illusory distillation": its apparent gains arise primarily from improved sampling efficiency rather than from acquiring genuinely new reasoning capabilities from the teacher.

</details>


### [166] [Air Quality Station Simulation via LSTM and Attention-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11839)
*Alexander Kostadinov,Petar O. Hristov,Dessislava Petrova-Antonova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SATADL的深度学习模型，用于在空气质量监测站发生故障或断电时，模拟输出长达48小时的多步超前空气质量预报（PM10浓度），作为虚拟代理站点保证数据连续性。模型在全球四组空气质量站点数据上进行验证，在决定系数和均方根误差上均优于多种基线和已发表的深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量监测站的硬件设备容易发生故障或断电，导致数据不可用，从而影响基于监测数据的整体分析质量。现有方法在应对长时间数据缺失时能力有限，需要一种能够准确模拟故障站点测量值的模型，在站点恢复运行前提供可靠的替代数据。

Method: 提出SATADL深度模型，其架构能够从数据的不同方面提取复杂关联信息，输出多步（最高48小时）超前空气质量预测。作者详细描述了模型架构及各组件，并在四组来自世界各地的空气质量站点数据上，通过模拟单个站点48小时内的假设性故障来验证模型性能，同时训练多种基线和已发表的深度学习模型作为基准进行对比。

Result: SATADL在不同预测窗口下均表现出优于基线和已有深度学习模型的性能，在决定系数（R²）和均方根误差（RMSE）两个指标上均取得更优结果，证明了其作为虚拟代理站点的适用性。

Conclusion: SATADL能够有效模拟故障监测站在长达48小时时段内的PM10浓度，为城市空气质量监测系统提供可靠的数据补全方案，确保在硬件故障期间数据分析和研究的连续性。

Abstract: Poor air quality in urban areas is driven by a complex chain of processes and presents a significant public health concern. To better understand and control the mechanisms that determine air quality, cities deploy networks of measurement stations, and launch initiatives for collecting denser data about the concentration of pollutants in the atmosphere. Extracting insights from the stations relies on their reliable and uninterrupted operation. However, hardware is susceptible to faults and black- outs that may result in data unavailability, which affects the overall quality of analyses. In this paper, we present a deep-learning model, called SATADL, which can infer complex relations and output multiple-hour-ahead air-quality forecasts. The goal of the model is to simulate the mea- surements of an unresponsive station until its operation is restored. The architecture of the model, which allows it to extract information from different aspects of the data, is described in detail and a careful examination of all of its components is provided. We demonstrate the performance of SATADL on four sets of air quality stations from around the world, by using it to simulate the concentration of PM10 for periods of hypothetical failures of one of the measurement stations, lasting for as long as 48 hours. A selection of baseline and published deep learning models were trained and used as a benchmark. The results show that SATADL per- forms better across different prediction windows, for both coefficient of determination and root mean squared error, demonstrating its suitability as a virtual proxy station.

</details>


### [167] [Small-Scale Experiments: Are We There Yet?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11859)
*Nicholas Lourie,Kyunghyun Cho,Karen Ullrich,Sanae Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文反驳了"小规模实验无法可靠预测大模型行为"的共识，指出混淆因素是超参数敏感性。小模型对超参数高度敏感，但这种敏感性随规模增大而减弱。作者通过消融实验证明充分调优的超参数比其他任何因素都重要，并揭示了超参数损失曲面随规模增大而维数降低的机理。基于此，作者提出了一种新的模型中心研究方法论，并成功从小规模实验恢复了Transformer中pre-normalization优于post-normalization这一此前需多年才能定论的大规模结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
